Twelve driver test groups and five image-grader groups pass on both Python runtimes. Thirty-two static entry-point checks pass. The driver requires the explicit image capability, the separate preflight reserve and a complete priced memory ledger, and validates the applied plan on image-session responses. Its sender now enforces the native one-MiB input-frame bound before writing. Complete-image grading requires the exact typed JSON answer and a terminal response. Every actual assistant answer and the original image remain in subsequent history; later correct answers cannot erase earlier failures. Verified reply-budget refusals are failed task outcomes, while crashes and framing failures invalidate execution.

The native source adds the distinct quantization-image-session-v1 kind. It preserves the text protocols, uses the owned candidate vision profile, reserves the entire reply including image expansion, and retains equality with actual HTTP usage. Image sessions have at most 32 requests and a 14.5-GB physical ceiling with the established six-GB preflight reserve; live headroom remains three GB. The shared metadata-only image counter expands the chosen plain or extended template without decoding pixels. Actual image preparation and generation still use the production HTTP handler. New native protocol-bound checks and actual image sessions have not run. No native build is started alongside the current model campaign.

Eight prepared conversations contain twenty-four user turns. Two existing photographs were visually reviewed before collecting answers, and deterministic bar/color rasters have explicit programmatic geometry. The bar rendering was also visually inspected. Questions cover basic recognition, color, counts, relative bar height and retaining the same image across follow-up turns. These are focused product fixtures, including familiar images already used by the repository; they are not unseen-photo evidence or statistical image noninferiority. Case SHA-256 is 097a94639133987db01cfcbed315e7bbbe9f0820556ca39fe04a86685af4039c.

No model answers or image-quality verdict exist. Freeze the complete native build/settings, paired execution and resource/acceptance protocol before inference, then retain every outcome and actual applied memory plan. Image-capacity and model-quality qualification remain independent gates. The ongoing final general-task campaign retains its original frozen binary and helpers unchanged.

Raw source, tests and complete prepared cases follow. Home paths are normalized to <HOME>; original and normalized hashes are retained, and compressed content is roundtrip-checked. Embedded image bytes are the existing public fixtures or generated test rasters, not model weights or logits.
